### Acceptance evidence the art side requires

Retained in `production/qa/evidence/chart-table/` (BLOCKING per the coding-standards UI and Visual rows); the criteria themselves are in Acceptance Criteria, groups D, F and G.

1. The chart at each `web.view` state, with no cost, progress, red, violet or distance label (AC-CT-25).
2. The depth-step banding check at 1080p and the Deck canvas, adjacent steps at least `RING_STEP_MIN_CHANNEL` per channel by pixel sampling (AC-CT-40, AC-CT-47).
3. A palette scan over the layer-3 screenshot set: zero Violet-band, zero Flesh-band, zero Alarm Red, zero loop-hue pixels (AC-CT-69).
4. The panel over a frozen world: world pixels outside the panel bit-identical to the pre-pause frame, layer 2 excluded (an active warning mark is held ON per HUD HS-23) (AC-CT-48).
5. Focus on both input paths (AC-CT-14); the hold bar at 0 / 50 / 100 % readable in a greyscale capture (AC-CT-37); the compact layout at 213.33 x 120 (AC-CT-30); the main menu, notice, UI-5 and UI-7 / 8 / 9 (AC-CT-78).
6. Motion and flashing *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; replaces "no element changing luminance faster than 3 Hz")*: the drift at or below the ceiling over 600 s with no periodic step (AC-CT-39); stopped under reduced motion; and a 60 s per-frame luminance log over the layer-3 rectangle and the frame mean, on a **bright lamp-lit world fixture** and on a **fixture with an active held-ON warning mark**, each including an Esc mash, a Chart mash and a held-direction run, scored with registry `flash_count` (`F <= 3`, HUD mark edges counted in the same capture), once with reduce flashing off and once on (AC-CT-82, AC-CT-114, AC-CT-115, AC-CT-116).
7. A greyscale pass over the chart and the panel: bracket, pick route, hold bar and `IN TRANSIT` distinguishable (AC-CT-75).

> **📌 Asset Spec** - Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:chart-table-web-map-and-menus` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions, and AI generation prompts from this section.

## Game Feel

*Requirement ids are `CT-GF-n`. No specialist wrote this section, so it is built from numbers already fixed elsewhere in this file or cited from their owners; every figure below names its source. Hold-timing figures use the registry value of `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` at authoring (0.8 s, owner: Player Controller) as an illustration in the manner of D.2, and are recomputed from the constant, never copied from this section. A UI layer has no "weight" in the combat sense; here weight is **stillness**: the layer feels like a heavy, static frame on which almost nothing moves, and the feel targets below are about response, restraint and the absence of ornament.*

### Feel statement

**CT-GF-1. The feel in one line.** Instant to answer, slow to move, never excited. Every input is answered in the frame it arrives or in the one after; nothing on the layer overshoots, bounces, pulses, shakes, glints or counts down (the three reading motions of ART 7.3 are absent, CT-2); the only autonomous motion is a web drifting at a fraction of one art pixel a second. The pause is a held breath, not a refuge (Player Fantasy, Moment 2): the panel appears without softening the world and disappears without ceremony.

**CT-GF-2. Feel pillars for this layer.** (1) *Answered*: no input is ever swallowed without a visible or documented reason (a disabled item is shown disabled; `SAVE_QUIT_WAIT` ignores input by rule, CT-21). (2) *Held*: the world beneath is the same frame (PIPE:265), so the panel's arrival changes nothing it does not cover. (3) *Deliberate*: the irreversible acts (UI-3 quit, UI-5 retire and delete) cost a full hold *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* on a linear bar, and the sortie commitment is off this layer entirely (the console press, CT-10). (4) *Plain*: no easing except the 100 ms bracket, no sound beyond a focus-move cue, a confirm cue and the queued-re-open acknowledgement (CT-3) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2/G6: CT-3 queued acknowledgement, ASK-CT-20)*.

### Responsiveness: the input-to-feedback chain

**CT-GF-3. Latency targets.** Frames are presentation frames of the root viewport; ticks are simulation ticks (`TICK_RATE` 60, so one tick is 16.67 ms). A target of "the same frame" means the first frame presented after the input event is processed.

| Input | First visible feedback | Target latency | Source and note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `chart_table` press, CLOSED to PANEL (SOLO) | panel plates begin their `CHART_FADE_MS` fade (or appear in a cut); bracket on Resume | **0 frames beyond the input frame** (presentation does not wait on the kernel) when no close occurred in the preceding `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS`; otherwise latched and applied at the interval end (**at most 334 ms, 1000 ms under reduce flashing**), plate and `request_pause` on the same frame *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | CT-3 (re-open governor), CT-4; the fade is 80-120 ms (ART 7.3), default 100 ms |
| Kernel confirms `PAUSED` after that press | none visible; `can_save_quit` becomes true | **at most 1 tick (16.67 ms)** after the request | KRN CR-15 (honoured at a tick boundary, P6); the fade hides the gap; Save & Quit stays disabled until then (EC-CT-30) |
| `chart_table` or Resume, panel to CLOSED | plates fade out (or cut); the closing press and its release are consumed | 0 frames beyond the input frame; world already live under SOLO on the next kernel tick (`request_resume`) | CT-41 (2) |
| Chart item accepted (panel to chart) | the panel is replaced by the full-bleed chart in one frame | **0 intermediate frames** (a cut) when the re-open governor admits the raise (no lower in the preceding interval; otherwise latched, CT-3); under reduce flashing, the long end of the fade band (120 ms) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | CT-3 |
| Direction press on the chart or panel | the bracket and bezel thickening start moving to the new target | 0 frames to start; **100 ms ease-out** to arrive; 0 ms (a cut) under reduced motion | ART 7.3; CT-6; the target position is final on the first frame, only the draw eases |
| Accept on a legal node | the dashed route appears, HOME to the node | **same frame** (no fade, a cut) | CT-7, CT-VA-8 |
| Accept on the picked node, on HOME at HOME, or on a non-selectable node | nothing changes (idempotent or inert) | same frame | EC-CT-5; there is deliberately no error feedback |
| Hold press edge on a destructive item | the fill starts from 0 | same frame | D.2, CT-22 |
| Hold reaches `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` (capped at `CT_HOLD_CAP_S`, 1.5 s) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6)* | the action fires on the first frame at or past the threshold (not on release) | at 60 fps: **frame 48** (800,016 microseconds against 800,000); at 30 fps: frame 25; at 10 fps (clamped to `UI_DT_MAX_MS` per frame): frame 16, i.e. 1.6 s of wall time | D.2; illustrative at the registry value |
| Delete hold reaches `DELETE_HOLD_S` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | the action fires on the first frame at or past 1,600,000 microseconds | at 60 fps: **frame 96** (1,600,032); at 30 fps: frame 49; at 10 fps (clamped): frame 32, i.e. 3.2 s of wall time | D.2; illustrative at the default |
| Hold released, cancelled, pointer exit, focus loss | the bar snaps to empty | **same frame**, no bounce | CT-22, TX-10 |
| Toggle on Display & Comfort (reduced motion, reduce flashing, display mode; master volume step, debounced like display mode) | the switch moves and its on / off word changes; the effect applies on the next frame | same frame for the control; **next frame** for the consumer; display mode needs the OS (the applied mode is read back and shown) | CT-18; the safety toggles are written synchronously, the display mode debounced 500 ms (`SETTINGS_WRITE_DEBOUNCE_MS`); invisible *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| Mouse hover | the item's label turns Tungsten; the bracket hides | same frame | CT-6 |
| Mouse click | activates on **pointer-up on the same item** that received the press; focus moves to it | same frame as the release | CT-6, EC-CT-16 |
| Device switch | all keycap glyphs swap to the new device's set | one frame, after `DEVICE_SWITCH_HYSTERESIS` (PCI, registry) | CT-6; keycap slots are fixed-width so nothing shifts |
| Save & Quit accepted | the four items enter their disabled styling; **nothing else** | same frame | CT-21; no spinner, bar or text |
| Continue / New campaign accepted | the items disable and the plate is removed in the same frame; layer-1 black; at least one full-black frame; then the restore call | 0 frames to black request; the black's own fade belongs to the Pipeline (`TRANSIT_HOLD`, PIPE:264) | CT-42; nothing is drawn between black and the first `RUNNING` frame |
| Panel or chart open to first presented frame, Deck class hardware | the first frame of the fade | one frame; no hitch above one frame on opening the chart (the layout is parsed at boot) | AC-CT-81, ADVISORY until the script budget is set |

**CT-GF-4. Mash tolerance** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; replaces "every press is honoured, no cooldown")*. Every **close** is honoured on its press frame and there is no cooldown on any panel item (Resume, Display & Comfort, Save & Quit, Back). A **re-open** pressed within `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS` (334 ms; 1000 ms under reduce flashing) of the last close is latched and applied when the interval elapses, plate and kernel pause together, and only `cancel` cancels it; a repeat `chart_table` press is idempotent *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (CT-3 re-open governor, EC-CT-68, EC-CT-77 to 79). The feel is therefore: closing is instant, opening after a recent close can lag by up to a third of a second (a press that is queued and then cancelled with `cancel` has no on-screen effect at all, which a tester must not file as a swallowed input; the queued press itself gives an audio-only acknowledgement, ASK-CT-20) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, never both a plate and a running sim wrongly, and ten presses in one second never produce more than three raises (at most six opposing transitions, `F <= 3` under registry `flash_count`; AC-CT-114, AC-CT-115). A same-boundary press pair converges (`PauseGate` re-issues the latest desired state, EC-CT-13); `chart_table` held with key echo toggles exactly once (EC-CT-10; the Adapter suppresses echo, CT-45).

**CT-GF-5. Input hygiene as feel.** The irreversible press is protected by feel rules, not by dialogs: a console press that opens the chart cannot also activate something (fresh press edge, CT-41 (1)); the `interact` that closes the layer on Resume is swallowed so the player standing at the pod console does not hop the instant they resume (CT-41 (2)); a click on the frozen world does nothing (CT-41 (4)); a held Esc does not flicker (CT-41 (3)). The player should never feel that the layer "did something I did not ask".

### Timing and motion

**CT-GF-6. Timing table.** The only timed behaviours on the layer.

| Behaviour | Duration or rate | Easing | Clock | Owner of the number |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Layer and panel fade, in and out | `CHART_FADE_MS`, default 100 ms, inside the 80-120 ms band; 120 ms under reduce flashing | linear (a fade; no easing specified by ART) | UI clock | band: ART 7.3; knob: this GDD, `[PLACEHOLDER]` |
| Re-open governor *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | a raise within `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS` (334 ms) of the last lower waits for the interval; 1000 ms (`CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS_REDUCED`) under reduce flashing; lowers are never delayed | none | UI clock | `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS` derived from registry `FLASH_MAX_PER_S`; reduced form `[PLACEHOLDER]`, this GDD |
| Panel to chart replacement | 0 ms (a cut); 120 ms fade under reduce flashing | none | UI clock | CT-3 |
| Bracket relocation | 100 ms | ease-out | UI clock | ART 7.3 (cited) |
| Bracket relocation under reduced motion | 0 ms | cut | n/a | HR-8; CT-13 |
| Hold-to-confirm fill | `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` (0.8 s at authoring; capped at `CT_HOLD_CAP_S`, 1.5 s) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6)*; `DELETE_HOLD_S` (1.6 s) for Delete *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | **linear**, no overshoot | UI clock, per-frame delta clamped to `UI_DT_MAX_MS` | Player Controller (registry) |
| Hold abort | 0 ms | cut to empty | UI clock | CT-22 |
| Filament drift | `FILAMENT_DRIFT_FRACTION` (0.5) of the cited ceiling of 1 art px a second = **0.5 art px a second**, in whole-art-px steps of `FILAMENT_DRIFT_STEP_PX` (1), stepped through an authored irregular interval table floored at one second (D.5): a mean of one visible step every 2 s, never a metronome *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, G2: creative-director lean, authored irregular step table)* | none (stepwise) | UI clock timer `acc_us` and index `i` | ceiling: ART 7.3; fraction and step: this GDD |
| Directional-navigation repeat | first repeat after `UI_NAV_REPEAT_MS` (350 ms), then every `UI_NAV_REPEAT_RATE_MS` (120 ms); held 1,000 ms gives 7 moves; no wrap | none | UI clock | this GDD, `[PLACEHOLDER]` |
| Settings write *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | the safety toggles and `notice_seen`: synchronous on the change; display mode and master volume: one write 500 ms after the last change *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; flushed at once on page close, Save & Quit, exit | n/a | UI clock | `SETTINGS_WRITE_DEBOUNCE_MS` (display mode and master volume) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, `[PLACEHOLDER]` |
| Load black | at least one presented full-black frame before the restore call | n/a | Pipeline | PIPE:264, CT-42 |

**CT-GF-7. Snap targets (positions are always whole pixels).** (1) Every layer-3 rectangle is positioned by anchors 0 or 1 plus integer HUD-px offsets (CT-16), so every bezel, bracket and rivet edge lies on a screen-pixel boundary at every `H`; a centred element is allowed only at an even on-screen width. (2) Panel edge: `W_panel` HUD px, `W_panel * H` screen px, an integer by construction, so the panel's left edge `P_w - W_panel * H` is an integer screen pixel (D.1: 1281, 1284, 854, 854, 854, 854 for the six canvas rows). (3) Node positions: authored integers in the 288 x 162 frame, scaled by the integer `m` (D.1b); a 24x24 glyph stays 24x24 at `H`. (4) The bracket relocates between integer positions; during the 100 ms ease its interpolated draw positions are rounded to whole HUD px so the bracket never sits on a half pixel. (5) Hold bar needle: `needle_px = min(floor(fill * L_BAR_HUD), L_BAR_HUD - 1)` whole HUD px (D.2); a `L_BAR_HUD` 64 bar over `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` (0.8 s, the Player Controller's registry value at authoring, illustrative) advances 80 art px a second, about 1.3 px a frame at 60 fps. (6) Filaments: whole-art-px offsets only, so the texture never rests on a half pixel (D.5). (7) Routes, seams and rule lines: 1-art-px quads aligned to whole art px, never width-1 line draws on a fractional transform (CT-11).

**CT-GF-8. Snap-to-node feel (gamepad and keyboard).** A direction press moves focus to the nearest focusable node whose bearing falls in the pressed direction's sector (`SNAP_SECTOR_DEG` 90 degrees), widening to the half-plane if the sector is empty, else staying (D.3). Feel targets: with three nodes, **no direction press is a dead control when another node exists on that side or in the half-plane** (the fallback exists for this); a press with no candidate leaves focus where it is with **no shake, no flash, no sound** (staying is silence, not an error); the bracket never wraps from the last node to the first; the same press always lands on the same node (deterministic, the diagonal assignment is exact, D.3); and every node is reachable from every other in at most N - 1 presses. Mouse and pad are equivalent in outcome: a click on a node is focus plus accept (CT-6). A stick resting on the deadzone boundary produces one move per edge, not a chatter (D.9, `MOVE_DEADZONE_EXIT`).

### Weight, juice and what is deliberately absent

**CT-GF-9. No juice, by rule.** No squash, scale pop, overshoot, bounce, wobble, shake, flash, glow, pulse, particle, screen-space effect, parallax, slide or wipe on any element of this layer (ART 7.3, rulings R16, CT-2). The press acknowledgement is a one-frame Paper White hold on the label (CT-VA-7). A destructive action looks like every other item until its bar fills. A completed hold fires on the same frame the bar is full; there is no celebratory beat. This is not austerity for its own sake: ornament on the truth layer reads as the layer reacting, and a reacting layer reads as an instrument that can be fooled.

**CT-GF-10. Stillness as weight.** The panel is a heavy opaque plate, chamfered and riveted (ART 7.4), that does not move once placed; the world beside it is a held frame, undimmed. The contrast (a still true plate beside a world frozen mid-lie at stage 3) is the feel the Player Fantasy aims at (Moment 2). The only life on the chart is the filament drift, kept deliberately below the art-bible ceiling so the screen reads "very low energy" (ART 2.11) and never "something is happening".

**CT-GF-11. Silence and absence of reward.** No sound on open, save or load; the only cues requested are a UI-bus focus-move cue, a confirm cue and the audio-only queued-re-open acknowledgement `UI_CHART_REOPEN_QUEUED` (CT-3, CT-29), owned by Adaptive Audio *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2/G6: CT-3 queued acknowledgement, ASK-CT-20)*. No "progress saved", no autosave mark, no completion flourish, no achievement toast, no tip on the black between menu and world (CT-36). Save & Quit's feel is *closing the log*: the items dim, the process ends.

### Feel risks and playtest observables

**CT-GF-12. What the playtest measures (all advisory, none a gameplay constant).**
- `CHART_TO_PRESS_S`: the median seconds from the first chart open of a sortie to the console press; target 20 s, flag above 40 s as the friction signal that Esc, Chart, pick, Esc, walk, press is too long. The reserved remedy is a separate chart action owned by the Player Controller, **never** data on the chart (Player Fantasy, validation 2; AC-CT-80).
- Whether the 100 ms bracket ease reads as responsive or laggy on a gamepad at the Deck's 60 Hz (the knob is cited from ART 7.3, not retuned here; a complaint is routed to the art bible).
- Whether `FILAMENT_DRIFT_FRACTION` 0.5 reads alive or dead (the first knob likely to move; Tuning Knobs, interaction notes).
- Whether the pause reads "held" and not "safe" (validation 3; AC-CT-106).
- The count of console presses made with a stale pick (a pick the player no longer remembers), per tester, and whether the text-free pick echo on the console plate was noticed (CT-10 item 5, AC-CT-124); a count above zero in two testers routes to Interaction and the UX spec, never to a timer that clears the pick *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- Whether a re-open delayed by the governor (up to 334 ms; 1000 ms under reduce flashing) is noticed or felt as a swallowed press (CT-3, CT-GF-4); the remedy is the AC-CT-115 capture and the interval, never removing the governor *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- Whether the hold at the registry value reads deliberate or sticky (a Controller-owned constant and the Alpha two-tap option (PCI:835)).

**CT-GF-13. Frame-time feel.** The layer must never be the cause of a visible hitch: the layout file is parsed at boot, not on open; the settings flush completes inside one frame (a few hundred bytes on the UI thread); per-frame work is the bracket tween, one accumulator or two and a handful of draw calls (D.10 estimates 26 in the MVP example and 33 at the maximum, against `LAYER3_DRAW_CALL_BUDGET` 40) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. The numeric script-time budget is `[PLACEHOLDER]` and measured in a spike on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (delegated ruling R12; AC-CT-45, AC-CT-81, ADVISORY until set). A large frame delta (a window drag, a display-mode change) is clamped to `UI_DT_MAX_MS` (50 ms) so no hold completes and no drift jumps from a hitch (EC-CT-58, D.2 example: a 400 ms frame advances a hold by 50 ms only).

**CT-GF-14. Cross-checks.** The feel numbers above are consistent with: ART 7.3 (fade band, bracket, drift ceiling, linear bar); HR-8 and HR-13 (reduced motion, 3 Hz); D.2, D.5, D.9 (hold, drift, repeat); CT-43 (the UI clock, independent of the kernel state so the bar and the drift run while the kernel is `PAUSED`); PCI:656 as clarified in C15.

## UI Requirements

*This system is a UI system: every surface listed here is its own. Requirement ids are `CT-UI-n`. The surfaces are layer-3 Controls in the root viewport (CT-1); the spec split follows rulings R30. `design/ux/` and `design/accessibility-requirements.md` do not exist yet, so every status below reads "not yet authored" and the shared-component grammar is defined by the HUD & Suit Layer's UX spec, which lands first (HS-48; `design/ux/interaction-patterns.md`); this layer cites it.*

Surfaces owned by this layer.** One row per screen, with the state that shows it (States and Transitions), the data it reads, its inputs, the spec that will own its pixels and the evidence it owes. Input columns use the actions of CT-5: navigate (direction), accept (`interact`), back (`cancel`), close (`chart_table`).

**CT-UI-2. Surfaces that are not this layer's.** (1) The pod-console prompt and its refusal words: Interaction, on the register plate (WM:1029); the layer only shares the grammar. (2) The failed-write notice (UI-10): HUD & Suit Layer (SL:1006). (3) Any suit, loop or gauge reading: HUD and In-World Instruments. (4) There is no ship-schematic console (IW-28, C4). (5) The first-`RUNNING`-frame instance of UI-7: HUD & Suit Layer. (6) Sounds: Adaptive Audio. (7) The full settings UI, rebinding, HUD scale, audio and accessibility settings: Settings & Input Rebinding and Accessibility Settings (Alpha).

### Shared components

**CT-UI-3. The component grammar this layer needs** (ART 7.2, 7.4; names provisional until `interaction-patterns.md` exists). (1) **Ground plate** with a chamfered Graphite-lit border and one rivet per corner. (2) **List item** (chamfered rectangle, Ground interior, normal and disabled). (3) **Focus bracket** plus bezel thickening (HR-11). (4) **Keycap** (chamfered, fixed-width slot, device-following glyph). (5) **Switch** (chamfered, position plus an on / off word). (6) **Three-choice list** (display mode: the current choice marked by shape plus a word). (7) **Hold-to-confirm bar** (linear, needle-mark). (8) **Message plate** (full-bleed Ground sized to text, 45-75 character reading measure, up to three items). (9) **Newest-record line** (instrument face, tabular numerals). (10) **Node glyph set and pod marker** (24x24). (11) **Back hint** (the text-labelled control that holds focus in view-only chart mode). Components 1, 2, 3, 4, 7 are shared with the HUD & Suit Layer's prompts and the Interaction hold bar; they are defined by the HUD & Suit Layer's UX spec, which lands first (HS-48); this layer cites them. The hold bar shares the HUD component's look only; on this layer an abort snaps to empty (CT-22, rulings R19), not the HUD's drain (`CONFIRM_DRAIN_RATE`, HS-42); a clarification row (C19) is routed to HUD / UX.

### Input and focus requirements

**CT-UI-4. Input map for the `chart_table` context** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Every input arrives as an Adapter-published MenuInput event (CT-45); the layer reads no device. Accept is the physical controls of `interact` in this context (E, gamepad A, Enter, Space; **never a mouse button**); back is `cancel` (X / B); close is `chart_table` (Esc / Start); directional navigation is stick, D-pad, arrow keys or WASD as four-way NAV events (CT-45), repeat per D.9. The Controller's `chart_table`-context note accepts the reuse and adds no action; the Enter and Space bindings and the LMB exclusion are in PC-2a. Mouse: the cursor is released (PC-2), hover and click as CT-6 through `POINTER` events, the pointer cursor changes over a selectable node, the wheel scrolls but nothing scrolls at MVP. Zoom edges never reach the layer (CT-5). Every flow completes on a gamepad alone (HR-14), including the chart snap and each destructive hold; every interactive control has an accessible name (CT-30).

**CT-UI-5. Default focus per surface.**

| Surface | Default focus | Why |
|---|---|---|
| S1 panel | Resume | non-destructive (CT-3) |
| S3 / S4 chart (spatial or compact) | HOME's node | never a target, so focus is never a recommendation (CT-10) |
| S5 chart, view-only | Back | nothing on the map is actionable (CT-15) |
| S6 Display & Comfort | the first control | TX-20 |
| S7 `UI3_PLATE` | Back (DOWNED entry) or Retry (`SAVE_FAILED` entry) | the non-destructive item (CT-21) |
| S9 notice *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | the dismiss item (never a toggle: no state-flipping control holds default focus on a screen reachable before the player's first deliberate input) | CT-20; AC-CT-44, AC-CT-131 |
| S10 main menu | Continue if enabled, else New campaign | CT-23 |
| S11 UI-5 | Back *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was Retire)* | non-destructive; a double-tap on New campaign must not retire a campaign (CT-24) |
| S13 UI-8 | New campaign | the one productive action; items are listed New campaign first, Quit second *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| S14 UI-9 | **New campaign** (items listed New campaign first, Quit second, as UI-8; default focus New campaign (Default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20), D83, OQ-CT-32) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*) | CT-25; never an overwrite; neither item destroys data *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| S10 main menu, Display & Comfort item | n/a | the item is reachable; default focus stays Continue (or New campaign when none), CT-23 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| S15 FAULTED-return menu | Continue | the newest verified generation is intact (CT-26) |

**CT-UI-6. Focus loss, device and window behaviour.** Focus is retained by item id across a live relayout (display-mode change, window resize); the bracket does not nudge and no animation plays (CT-18). A window focus loss aborts any hold fill, clears navigation state and keeps the layer open (CT-41 (5)). A gamepad disconnect keeps the layer open and switches glyphs to the remaining device (EC-CT-22).

### Layout and scaling requirements

**CT-UI-7. Canvases.** The layer is laid out against the six reachable canvas rows (CT-16, D.1): 640 x 360, 320 x 180, 426.67 x 266.67, 213.33 x 133.33, 426.67 x 240 and 213.33 x 120 HUD px (the smallest). Anchor-relative only, 5 % safe margin rounded up to whole HUD px (registry `safe_margin`), panel `W_panel = P_w div (3 * H)`, chart frame 288 x 162 at multiplier `m`, compact iff `m < 1` (rows 4 and 6). Nothing scrolls at MVP. Panel labels are never truncated or ellipsised (CT-28); the fit lint runs against the real faces.

**CT-UI-8. Two scales on two sibling layers** *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0008 sync: pixel face layer 3, reading face layer 4; ADR-0008 s2.5)*. The instrument face and all glyphs scale by the integer `H`; the reading face scales continuously (the only non-integer text, ART 7.1), so plates that carry reading-face text are sized to the text with a minimum and the full-bleed message plate carries every paragraph (CT-3, CT-21). -> ADR candidate (b): the two-scale mechanism and the shared theme.

**CT-UI-9. Strings.** Every user-visible string is a key in the closed `ct.*` table with a fit class (`LABEL_1`, `LABEL_2`, `PARAGRAPH`), owned by this layer, ship-log register, Save/Load's vocabulary only (CT-28); the lexicon of Player Fantasy "Tone and language" applies. Final copy is the UX spec's and the narrative director's; stale-token lint with a signed allow-list (AC-CT-68).

### Accessibility requirements (MVP, structural)

**CT-UI-10. What is required now (CT-30, rulings R27).** Opaque Ground plates (HR-2); focus as a shape (HR-11); ART 7.1 type and contrast floors (HR-12), met by the node outlines, the dashed ORPHAN outline and the Hull Grey disabled style (R-AD-4 and R-AD-5 closed, OQ-CT-24 closed; decorative frames and item borders are a signed FRAME role in AC-CT-75c); gamepad completeness (HR-14); no colour-only state (SL:1010; every state in CT-11 has a shape or word); an accessible name on every control (a chart node's is its neutral ordinal, nothing drawn, CT-30) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; reduced motion and reduce flashing available from the first launch (the notice, the Display & Comfort page); no true-value overlay as an accessibility option (ART 7.4); no Attention, sanity, horror or threat element under any setting (CT-31, CT-32, CT-34). **Deferred to Alpha** (Accessibility Settings / Settings): AccessKit wiring (NOT SOURCEABLE in the engine reference), hold-duration and two-tap option, HUD opacity floor, reading-face scale, HUD scale. The project's accessibility tier is **standard** (Default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20): D80, OQ-CT-8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.

### Cross-surface rules

**CT-UI-11. What no surface may contain** (each a CT rule with an acceptance criterion): an Attention element (CT-31), a horror element (CT-32), a loot or salvage value (CT-33), a sanity element (CT-34), a fuel, time, ETA, danger or recommendation (CT-35), a save decoration (CT-36), an inventory (CT-37), title art, a tagline or a logo animation (CT-23; a plain reading-face game title is allowed, CT-VA-21), a thumbnail, a completion figure or a wall-clock "last played" (CT-23), a spinner, tip or percentage at load (CT-36, CT-42), and the word "Paused" (CT-17).

**CT-UI-12. Reserved slots (CT-38).** Crew glyphs and left-edge chat (VS-B), Codex annotations (VS), post-loss UI (VS-A), Onboarding, the beam picker, the Records page and un-retire (Alpha): no stub art and no layout space at MVP; each is one row in CT-38 and one review check (AC-CT-74).

> **📌 UX Flag — Chart Table (Web Map & Menus)**: This system has UI requirements. In Phase 4 (Pre-Production), run `/ux-design` to create a UX spec for each screen or HUD element this system contributes to **before** writing epics: `design/ux/chart-table.md` (the spatial and compact chart, the pick, view-only mode), `design/ux/pause-panel.md` (the panel, Display & Comfort, Save & Quit, `UI3_PLATE`, the hold-to-confirm bar) and `design/ux/main-menu.md` (the first-launch notice, Continue / New campaign including UI-5, UI-7 / UI-8 / UI-9 and the FAULTED return), with the shared components (Ground plate, list item, bracket, keycap, switch, hold bar, message plate) in `design/ux/interaction-patterns.md`, defined by the HUD & Suit Layer's UX spec, which lands first (HS-48); this layer cites them. Stories that reference UI should cite `design/ux/[screen].md`, not the GDD directly. Retained screenshots of each screen, at sanity stage 0 and forced stage 3 wherever the screen is reachable in a session, in `production/qa/evidence/chart-table/` are the evidence gate (AC-CT-25, AC-CT-30, AC-CT-78).

## Cross-References

Every explicit dependence on another document's specific element, as read by `/review-all-gdds` Phase 2c. *Nature* is one of Data dependency (we consume their output), State trigger (their state change triggers our behaviour), Rule dependency (our rule assumes theirs is also true) or Ownership handoff (we hand a value to them). A number this GDD cites and does not own is listed with its owner; this GDD states no second value for any of them. The Formulas, Edge Cases, Tuning Knobs, Acceptance Criteria, Visual/Audio, Game Feel and UI Requirements sections introduce no neighbour dependency beyond the tables below and cites the same targets by the same names; the additional sources it reads are: the UI palette hexes (art bible 4.1, 4.6, inlined in Visual/Audio and the AC fixtures), ART 4.0 principles 2 and 3 and 8.11 flag 1 (depth steps), HR-5 and HR-8 (reduced motion), the `modules/ui.md` and `modules/input.md` engine references (dual focus, key echo NOT SOURCEABLE), the registry constants listed in Tuning Knobs, and the qa-lead's flags F-A to F-G (Acceptance Criteria, "Gaps").

## Acceptance Criteria

*Ids `AC-CT-1` to `AC-CT-139` (plus 47b, 75b-75d) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; every id appears in the coverage matrix (the generated check of AC-CT-133). Numbering is append-only: criteria cited by number in rule and formula text (D.1b to AC-CT-26, D.4 and EC-CT-9 to AC-CT-24, D.6 to AC-CT-47, D.10 to AC-CT-45, CT-4 and EC-CT-73 to AC-CT-2) keep their number; the qa-lead's corrections were applied to the criteria in place, and new criteria take 82 onward. Each criterion names the rule, formula, transition or edge case it traces to; a coverage matrix closes the section. "Without opening another document" is the standard: every term a tester needs is defined in the glossary or the fixtures below.*

### Gate convention, tags and story types

**Tags.** `(auto)` = automated unit or integration test with a test double, deterministic, no wall clock. `(lint)` = a static or content lint run in CI. `(review)` = a named reviewer reads a retained artefact against a checklist. `(manual)` = a person plays or inspects, with retained evidence. `(spike)` = measured once on the real display path and real faces, result retained. `(integration)` = run against the real neighbour modules. `(playtest)` = outside testers. `(auto-render)` or `(render)` = an automated render-capture criterion run by the **auto-render job** on a rendering-capable runner (not `--headless`, which cannot read back viewports), compared by pixel mask or numeric log and retained *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Each tag carries its **story type** after a comma (Logic, Integration, UI, Visual, Config), which is how `/story-readiness` resolves the gate level from the coding-standards table.

**Gate levels (applied once, here).** `(auto)` Logic criteria and `(integration)` criteria are **BLOCKING**. `(lint)` and `(auto-render)` criteria are BLOCKING; **a lint or render AC marked "CI job to be built" is not verifiable until that job exists, the job is its own story and a declared dependency of every story that cites the criterion, and a missing job makes the story BLOCKED, never passed** (the source scans, fit-class measurement, layout-JSON lint, string scans, asset-name scan, scene-id whitelist, palette lint and the auto-render job are all new; gap F-E; the test doubles, hooks and gdUnit4 setup are gap F-H) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the "BLOCKING once the job exists" escape hatch is removed)*. `(manual)` UI and Visual criteria marked BLOCKING need a **retained screenshot** in `production/qa/evidence/chart-table/`: a screenshot captured and discarded is an assertion, not evidence. `(review)` criteria are BLOCKING when they gate a UI or Visual story, and say so. `(spike)` and `(playtest)` are **ADVISORY** unless stated.

**Sanity stages.** Screens reachable in a session (panel, chart, Display & Comfort, `UI3_PLATE`, the `FAILED` panel) are captured at **sanity stage 0 and forced stage 3** (`force_stage`, AC-SN-83). **Boot-flow screens** (notice, main menu, UI-5, UI-7 / UI-8 / UI-9, the load hold) exist when the kernel is not `RUNNING`, so no session, no Sanity and no `force_stage` exist: they are captured **once** (stage "n/a"), plus a structural lint that boot-flow scenes reference no Sanity, Filter or Attention symbol and are not children of the Filter's viewport (AC-CT-46). The FAULTED-return menu is reachable after a session and is captured with `force_stage` 3 active before the fault. *(orchestrator ruling: qa-lead R3; the draft's "both stages" for pre-session screens was physically unsatisfiable. Whether `force_stage` should work pre-session is OQ-CT-18.)*

### Glossary (terms the criteria use)

`HOME` = the home-ship node; `HOME_ID` = the `derelict_id` of the `web.view.nodes[]` entry whose `kind` is the home ship, never a constant (K16). `ChartPick` = the layer's host-local pending target, NONE or one `derelict_id` (CT-7). **Legal target** = a member of `legal_targets(view)` (D.4). `PANEL`, `CHART`, `CHART_VIEW_ONLY`, `DISPLAY_COMFORT`, `SAVE_QUIT_WAIT`, `SAVE_FAILED`, `UI3_PLATE`, `HOLD_CONFIRM`, `UI5_NEW_OVER_EXISTING`, `MAIN_MENU`, `BOOT_NOTICE`, `LOAD_HOLD`, `CLOSED` = the layer's states (States and Transitions). `UI-3`, `UI-5`, `UI-7`, `UI-8`, `UI-9` = the Save/Load surfaces of SL:998-1005 as this layer draws them (UI-3 = `UI3_PLATE`; UI-5 = `UI5_NEW_OVER_EXISTING`). `can_save_quit` = `PauseGate`'s one boolean (CT-4). `PauseGate` = the single adapter to the kernel control plane. `H` = HUD integer scale; `k` = integer world scale (never the zoom step); "row 1 to 6" = the canvas rows of the table below. "WM-36 list" = the `web.view` field list, exactly the seven fields of AC-WM-60: `nodes[]` {`derelict_id`, `template_id`, `kind`, `ring`, `world_slot`, `status`, `unknown_seen`, `loaded`, `berthed`}, `pod_at`, `transit_state`, `destination_ready`, `leg_target_id`, `leg_origin_id`, `leg_dir`. The layer reads no field outside this list *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G2)*. `IN_FLIGHT` = the `pod_at` value while the pod is between nodes. `[UNV]` = unverified against the engine or real faces.

**The six canvas rows (D.1, D.1b), inlined.** `P_w x P_h` is the picture in screen px; the HUD canvas is `P_w / H` by `P_h / H`.

| Row | Picture, mode, `k`, `H` | Canvas (HUD px) | `W_panel` | `safe_x` / `safe_y` | `content_w` | `V_avail` | `m` | Layout |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1920x1080 keep, 3, 3 (same canvas: 2560x1440 at `H` 4, 3840x2160 at `H` 6) | 640 x 360 | 213 | 32 / 18 | 177 | 324 | 2 | spatial |
| 2 | 1920x1080 keep, 3, 6 | 320 x 180 | 106 | 16 / 9 | 86 | 162 | 1 | spatial |
| 3 | 1280x800 expand (Deck), 2, 3 | 426.67 x 266.67 | 142 | 22 / 14 | 116 | 238 | 1 | spatial |
| 4 | 1280x800 expand, 2, 6 | 213.33 x 133.33 | 71 | 11 / 7 | 56 | 119 | 0 | compact |
| 5 | 1280x720 keep, 2, 3 | 426.67 x 240 | 142 | 22 / 12 | 116 | 216 | 1 | spatial |
| 6 | 1280x720 keep, 2, 6 | 213.33 x 120 (smallest) | 71 | 11 / 6 | 56 | 108 | 0 | compact |

**Fixture constants** (cited from their owners so no criterion needs another document; none is a new value). `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` 0.8 s (Player Controller, registry; range 0.6-1.9). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)* Drift ceiling 1 art px a second (ART 7.3). `DEVICE_SWITCH_HYSTERESIS` 250 ms (PCI, registry). Fade band 80-120 ms and bracket ease-out 100 ms (ART 7.3); `CHART_FADE_MS` 100. `UI_DT_MAX_MS` 50; `UI_NAV_REPEAT_MS` 350 / `UI_NAV_REPEAT_RATE_MS` 120; `RING_STEP_MIN_CHANNEL` 3; `S_MIN` 32; `SETTINGS_WRITE_DEBOUNCE_MS` 500; `L_BAR_HUD` 64; `CHART_FRAME` 288 x 162. Colours (ART 4.1, 4.6): Ground `#1C2128`, Void Steel `#0B1018`, Graphite lit `#5C6670`, Graphite mid `#2A303A`, Hull Grey `#8C949C`, Paper White `#DDE4EA`, Tungsten fixture `#FFCC66`, Alarm Red `#E0102A`; Violet hue 270-330 degrees with saturation above 25 %; Flesh hue 5-30 degrees, saturation 20-60 %, value 45-85 %.

**Test doubles assumed.** `ViewDouble` (feeds a `web.view` snapshot and records every call made on it, so a call to `web.leg_timing()` or a read of a progress field is observable). `KernelDouble` (records `request_pause`, `request_resume`, `request_shutdown`; exposes a settable state RUNNING / PAUSED / FAULTED / BOOTING and a count of `submit` calls). `SaveDouble` (scripted `SaveInfo`, `save_status`, `campaign_info()`, `restore_reason`, and the results `SL_QUIT_NO_WRITE_DOWNED`, `SL_QUIT_DEDUPED`, `SL_LOAD_NO_LOADABLE_SAVE`, incompatible-build; a scripted variant that publishes `quit_write_active`, then `FAILED` + `quit_held` at `T_cfg`, and a silent variant for the no-timer check). `ClockDouble` (drives the layer's UI delta in integer microseconds). `StoreDouble` (the `SettingsStore` seam; missing, corrupt or read-only). `WindowDouble` (display-mode requests, refusing fullscreen on demand). `InteractionDouble` (the console seam). Every double used in a cross-system criterion is kept honest by AC-CT-109 (contract tests).

### A. Architecture, registrations and the pause answer

- **AC-CT-1** (auto, Logic) [CT-27] GIVEN the project's registries after boot with the layer loaded, WHEN the state-block, hook, command and mutator registries are enumerated, THEN no entry has the Chart Table as owner and no type introduced by the layer carries `allowed_when_paused` (the paused-allowed list of KRN CR-16 is unchanged).
- **AC-CT-2** (auto + lint CI job to be built, Logic) [CT-4, KRN Open Question 6, checkpoint Q17] GIVEN a `KernelDouble` fixed at RUNNING (never PAUSED), WHEN the full chart flow runs (open, move focus across every node, pick, close, open again with the pick kept), THEN every step behaves identically to the same flow with the kernel PAUSED, **and** a lint over the layer's sources finds no reference to `Kernel.state`, `State.PAUSED` or `is_paused()` outside `pause_gate.gd` (symbol names to be confirmed by the lead-programmer; the exact symbol list is a **prerequisite recorded in gap F-E** before this lint can run *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*). Pinned wording: **no rule reads `PAUSED`; the chart is functional with the kernel RUNNING.**
- **AC-CT-3** (lint CI job to be built + auto, Logic) [CT-27, WM AC-WM-88 / AC-WM-109] GIVEN the layer's sources, WHEN scanned for the kernel's command-submission call (symbol name to be confirmed by the lead-programmer and recorded as a prerequisite in gap F-E *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*), THEN no submit path originates in the layer; **and** GIVEN a `KernelDouble`, WHEN a full session through every state of the machine runs, THEN the submit count is 0 and the only kernel calls are `request_pause`, `request_resume` and `request_shutdown` (TX-26, and TX-30 from any Quit, boot-flow included). The layer cannot cause a command except through the console press, which is Interaction's.
- **AC-CT-4** (auto, Logic) [CT-15, CT-27, CT-35, WM:300] GIVEN a `ViewDouble` that records calls, WHEN every chart state is exercised (HOME, UNSEEN, SEEN, BOARDED, `unknown_seen`, `berthed`, ABSORBED, ORPHAN, `pod_at` HOME / derelict / IN_FLIGHT, LEG, ARRIVING, BERTHING), THEN `web.leg_timing()` is never called and every field read belongs to the WM-36 list (the seven fields above; `leg_target_id`, `leg_origin_id` and `leg_dir` are endpoint identity, never progress); **and** GIVEN the `ViewDouble` in LEG with `leg_dir` OUT and then BACK, THEN the recorded reads include `leg_target_id` and `leg_dir` (and `leg_origin_id` on the BACK leg) and nothing outside the list; a read of a field outside the list fails the test; **and** GIVEN a view with an injected field named like a progress datum (elapsed ticks, remaining ticks, `t0`, `n`), THEN no drawn element changes *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G2)*.
- **AC-CT-5** (auto, Logic) [CT-4, TX-13, TX-14] GIVEN mode SOLO, the layer CLOSED and no close in the preceding `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS`, WHEN `chart_table` is pressed, THEN the layer is in PANEL on that frame, `request_pause` is called exactly once on that frame and default focus is Resume; GIVEN mode OVERLAY, THEN the same panel opens with **zero** kernel calls *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: "on that frame" holds when no close occurred inside the governor interval; inside it, AC-CT-114)*.
- **AC-CT-6** (auto, Logic) [CT-4, EC-CT-13, ASK-CT-9] GIVEN SOLO and a close followed by a press, WHEN a `request_pause` and a `request_resume` reach one kernel boundary (a `KernelDouble` that applies them in arrival order and ends `RUNNING` with no service cycle, as CR-15 now specifies), THEN the layer's `open` boolean equals the kernel's final pause state within two ticks and no half-open state persists (`PauseGate` re-issues the latest desired state); with the re-open governor a double press within the interval after a close (close at t, presses at t+100 ms and t+200 ms, `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS` 334, or the same scaled to 1000 ms under reduce flashing) queues one raise and the second press is idempotent (CT-3, AC-CT-114c): no kernel call is made before t+334 ms (t+1000 ms), then exactly one `request_pause` is issued with the plate, no `request_resume` follows the close, and the layer ends open with the kernel PAUSED (the double-Esc of Moment 2 ends paused, never running with a closed layer); only `cancel` clears the queue and sends nothing *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G4: conforms to CT-3 and AC-CT-114c)*, while a double press from a quiet start is an immediate open then an immediate close, the same-boundary pair this criterion covers. *(ASK-CT-9 answered by CR-15 as amended 2026-10-27; proven against the real kernel in AC-CT-109.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-CT-7** (auto, Logic) [CT-3, R2; qa-lead R1; Q-B ruling] GIVEN every combination of {`can_save_quit` true or false} x {`save_status` IDLE / WRITTEN / FAILED} x {`transit_state` IDLE / LEG / ARRIVING / BERTHING} x {a `SaveDouble` that returns `SL_QUIT_NO_WRITE_DOWNED` or not} x {state PANEL or `SAVE_QUIT_WAIT`}, WHEN the panel is drawn, THEN the items are Resume, Chart, Display & Comfort, Save & Quit in that order and none is absent; Resume, Chart and Display & Comfort are enabled in PANEL for every combination (**Chart is enabled while `transit_state != IDLE`: it opens view-only, TX-17**; *orchestrator ruling Q-B*); Save & Quit is enabled iff `can_save_quit` is true; in `SAVE_QUIT_WAIT` all four are disabled; a disabled item is drawn in the disabled style of CT-VA-7 (Hull Grey `#8C949C` label and dashed border; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: it was Graphite lit `#5C6670`, 2.77:1) and activating it does nothing.

- **AC-CT-10** (auto + manual, Logic) [CT-5, CT-22, TX-10, EC-CT-11] GIVEN a hold fill at 50 %, WHEN each of release, `cancel`, `chart_table`, pointer exit and window focus loss occurs in turn, THEN in each case the bar is empty in the same frame, the state returns to the screen that owns the item (`UI3_PLATE` or `UI5_NEW_OVER_EXISTING`), no other state changes, and the layer is still open; a second `chart_table` then closes it; **the pointer path** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN `POINTER{DOWN}` on Delete, Retire or Quit without saving held for the full `HOLD_S`, THEN the action fires once with no key or pad input, and GIVEN a `POINTER{UP}` before the threshold, THEN the bar is empty and nothing fires.

### B. The pick, the mirror and the console seam

- **AC-CT-16** (auto, Logic) [CT-7, EC-CT-5] GIVEN the pod at HOME, `transit_state` IDLE and legal targets {A, B}, WHEN accept lands on A, THEN `ChartPick = A`; accepting A again leaves it A; accepting B replaces it; accepting HOME clears it; accepting an ORPHAN node changes nothing (a `berthed` record empties the set and cannot be focused, AC-CT-132) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **AC-CT-17** (auto, Logic) [CT-8, EC-CT-1, EC-CT-2] GIVEN `ChartPick = A`, WHEN (1) a `request_restore` occurs for each of `NEW_GAME`, `LOAD_GAME`, `DEATH_STUB`, `FAULT_RECOVERY`, (2) `pod_at` leaves IN_FLIGHT (arrival), (3) A becomes ABSORBED, (4) A becomes ORPHAN, (5) A becomes `berthed`, (6) A is absent from `web.view`, THEN `ChartPick = NONE` in each case on that change; **and** the bracket does not move unless the focused node itself left the focusable set (then focus is on HOME); **and** no message is shown.
- **AC-CT-18** (auto + auto-render, Logic + Visual) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* [CT-8, CT-15, EC-CT-24] GIVEN `transit_state` in {LEG, ARRIVING, BERTHING} (and separately `pod_at == IN_FLIGHT`), WHEN the chart opens or is already open, THEN the layer is `CHART_VIEW_ONLY`, no node is focusable or activatable, focus is on Back, the pick cannot change, and **no element is drawn from `ChartPick`** (**Logic half, headless:** the layer's draw list, exposed by the draw-list hook of gap F-H, is identical with the pick set before the departure and with it NONE; **render half, auto-render:** the two captures are identical within the layer-3 mask; the in-flight route itself is drawn from `leg_target_id` / `leg_origin_id` / `leg_dir`, AC-CT-130) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **AC-CT-19** (auto with an `InteractionDouble`, Integration) [CT-10 table, TX-18, EC-CT-3, EC-CT-4, EC-CT-80; accepted seam PCI Interactions row 17 and IA-40] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: split into 19a to 19d; the stale "degraded" clause is dropped)*. **19a** GIVEN the pod at HOME, nothing berthed, `transit_state` IDLE: WHEN the POD prompt is pressed with a valid pick, THEN the injected command is `pod_hop{target_id := ChartPick}`; WHEN pressed with no valid pick and exactly one legal target, THEN the chart opens with focus on HOME, no command is submitted and no refusal is produced. **19b** GIVEN the pod at a derelict: WHEN the POD prompt is pressed, THEN the command carries HOME and `ChartPick` is not read. **19c** GIVEN a record B berthed: WHEN the DOCK prompt is pressed with `ChartPick` NONE, A, or an invalid value, THEN the command is `unberth{}` with no payload, the pick is neither read nor changed and the chart does not open (**the unberth case**); WHEN the POD prompt is pressed, THEN with any pick or none no chart opens, no target-bearing `pod_hop` is injected and Web Map's `BERTH_PENDING` words are requested (R6) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **19e** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the pod at a derelict: WHEN the DOCK prompt is pressed (if exposed), THEN no command and no chart occur (R1b), and WHEN the pod is at HOME with nothing berthed and a valid pick, THEN the DOCK press injects `berth{ChartPick}` (R4a; see AC-CT-95). **19d** GIVEN `transit_state` in {LEG, ARRIVING, BERTHING}: WHEN either prompt is pressed, THEN no command and no chart open occur (the refusal is Web Map's). The table of CT-10 is loaded as data and every row R1 to R8 (including R1b and R4a, cross-cited to AC-CT-95) is covered by one case *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.

### C. Layout, fit and compact layout

- **AC-CT-28** (auto + lint CI job to be built, Logic) [D.1, CT-16] GIVEN row 6 (213.33 x 120), THEN `V_need = 83 <= V_avail = 108` with `n_wrap = 0`; WHEN the `ct.*` fit-class assignment yields `n_wrap > 3` at row 6, THEN the lint fails.
- **AC-CT-29** (lint CI job to be built, Config) [CT-16, CT-11; qa-lead R11] GIVEN every layer-3 scene, THEN every anchor is 0 or 1 (or belongs to a centred element whose on-screen width is an even number of screen pixels at every reachable `H`) and every offset is an integer HUD px; **and** every route and seam quad has whole-art-px position and size, and the layer's draw code contains no `draw_line` of width 1 on a transformed canvas item.

- **AC-CT-31** (auto, Logic) [CT-44; qa-lead R10] GIVEN the compact list with records in layout-file order, WHEN a record becomes SEEN, BOARDED, `berthed`, ORPHAN or ABSORBED, THEN the relative order of the remaining rows never changes, an ORPHAN row stays in its slot (non-selectable), an ABSORBED record leaves the list, and an UNSEEN record's label has the same form as a SEEN record's. The order is never sorted by status, distance, fuel, danger, recency or seen / unseen. In the compact list the planned-sortie relation is a Tungsten dashed rule under the picked row and no other mark. A record's ordinal key is unchanged by every one of those status changes (AC-CT-112).
- **AC-CT-32** (lint CI job to be built, Config) [D.8, CT-28] GIVEN every `ct.*` key, each of the six rows and the real faces (the estimate `ADV_EM` is replaced by measured widths; **NOT SOURCEABLE until the reading face is imported**), THEN every `LABEL_1` fits one line, every `LABEL_2` at most two, with `TEXT_EXPANSION_TEST` applied; no label is truncated or ellipsised at runtime; the closed set of instrument-face strings contains only `IN TRANSIT`, numerals, keycap letters and short state words.
- **AC-CT-33** (lint CI job to be built, Config) [CT-12, D.1b, D.3, EC-CT-54, EC-CT-55] GIVEN `chart_layout.json`, THEN every record in the roster has a position, a `home_attach` offset (derelicts) and a stable ordinal *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; the attach, inset and composite lints are AC-CT-121)*; no position is computed from `world_slot`, legs, ticks or distance; every pairwise centre distance between focusable records is `>= S_MIN` (32); the authored distances show no correlation with any Web Map & Transit cost value; the snap graph over the layout is strongly connected; WHEN an entry is removed, THEN the lint fails the build and the runtime falls back to slot `(ordinal - 1) mod N_FALLBACK_SLOTS` with a log line; WHEN a roster cannot fit the compact list, THEN the lint fails the build and the row order is not changed to fit.
- **AC-CT-34** (auto, Logic) [D.3, CT-44] GIVEN the D.3 example layout (HOME (144, 100), A (210, 60), B (80, 40)) and `SNAP_SECTOR_DEG` 90, THEN from HOME: right gives A, up gives A (fallback), left gives B, down stays; from A: left gives HOME; a node exactly on a diagonal belongs to exactly one sector; the result is independent of the order candidates are listed in; no direction wraps.
- **AC-CT-35** (auto, Logic) [D.9] GIVEN a direction held for 300 ms and 1,000 ms with 350 / 120, THEN `n_moves` is 1 and 7; a held direction at the last node produces no wrap; a stick on the deadzone boundary produces one move per edge.

### D. Time, hold, motion and colour

- **AC-CT-36** (auto, Logic) [D.2, CT-22, CT-43; qa-lead R4] GIVEN a `ClockDouble` and `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` at its registry value 0.8 s (threshold 800,000 microseconds), THEN (a) at `dt_us` 16,667 the action fires on frame 48 and not frame 47 (`t_us` 800,016 against 783,349); (b) at `dt_us` 33,333 it fires on frame 25 (frame 24 = 799,992); (c) at `dt_us` 100,000 (10 fps) each frame adds 50,000 microseconds (clamped), so it fires on frame 16, i.e. 1.6 s of wall time, twice the nominal hold (accepted by D.2); (c2) the same run for the Delete hold fires on frame 96 at 60 fps, frame 49 at 30 fps and frame 32 (3.2 s wall) at 10 fps *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; (d) repeating (a) with `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` 0.6 s fires at frame 36 and with 1.2 s at frame 72; (e) one 400 ms frame advances `t_us` by exactly 50,000; (f) with the `KernelDouble` PAUSED the bar still advances (the UI clock runs while the kernel is paused); (g) with `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` 1.9 s the quit / Retire hold fires at frame 90 (60 fps; 1,500,030 us) and frame 46 (30 fps, `dt_us` 33,333: frame 45 = 1,499,985 < 1,500,000), the Delete hold at frame 114 (60 fps) and frame 58 (30 fps: frame 57 = 1,899,981 < 1,900,000), and the two thresholds differ by 400,000 us *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6)*

### E. Saving, quitting and campaigns

- **AC-CT-50** (auto + auto-render, Logic + Visual) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* [CT-21, TX-21, TX-22, K1; qa-lead R2; Q-C ruling] GIVEN `can_save_quit` true and a `SaveDouble` that does not return `SL_QUIT_NO_WRITE_DOWNED`, WHEN Save & Quit is accepted, THEN `save_and_quit()` is called once; all four items are disabled; `chart_table` and `cancel` are ignored; the **draw-list difference** between the frame before the accept and the first frame after (Logic half, headless) is **confined to the four item entries** (their disabled styling and the removed bracket: with every item disabled nothing is focusable, *orchestrator ruling Q-C*), and in the **render half** (auto-render, masked to the panel plate rectangle so the world strip, which is frozen, is excluded) the pixel difference is confined to the four item rectangles; no pixel inside the mask but outside those rectangles changes, and no element, glyph, text run or animation exists that was not in the PANEL frame (no spinner, ring, bar, percentage, toast or text change); and the layer makes **no** `request_shutdown` call on this path (a `SaveDouble` that ends the process after `WRITTEN` and one that skips an unchanged-hash write produce the same layer behaviour).
- **AC-CT-51** (auto, Logic) [CT-21, CT-4, EC-CT-30] GIVEN `can_save_quit` false (the first tick after opening, OVERLAY without Session's form, or a confirmation pending), THEN Save & Quit is disabled (it stays focusable but inert) and `save_and_quit()` is never called.
- **AC-CT-52** (auto + manual, UI, BLOCKING) [CT-21, TX-24, EC-CT-29] GIVEN a `SaveDouble` that returns `SL_QUIT_NO_WRITE_DOWNED`, THEN nothing is written, `UI3_PLATE` opens with Back as default focus and the string stating that the newest autosave will be used, `cancel` returns to PANEL, `chart_table` closes (SOLO resume); and the string set contains no death language (SL:993).
- **AC-CT-53** (auto + manual, UI, BLOCKING) [CT-21, TX-23, TX-25, TX-32, K3; qa-lead R19; Q-F and Q-G rulings] GIVEN `save_status == FAILED`, or a `SaveDouble` that publishes `FAILED` and `quit_held` at the configured timeout (`SAVE_QUIT_TIMEOUT_MS`, Save/Load's; gap F-D) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6)*, THEN the layer is the PANEL with the plain word `FAILED` in the panel's (otherwise empty) status-line slot (CT-17; the panel carries no playtime to replace, *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*) (*orchestrator ruling Q-F*; a word, not colour alone, not Alarm Red), the four items are in CT-3 order, Resume, Chart, Display & Comfort and `chart_table` work; accepting Save & Quit opens `UI3_PLATE` with Retry (default focus) and Quit without saving (hold); Retry returns to `SAVE_QUIT_WAIT`; repeated failure shows no counter and no escalation text; GIVEN `can_save_quit` false on that plate, THEN Retry is shown **disabled, not hidden** (*orchestrator ruling Q-G*) and no `save_and_quit()` call is made.
- **AC-CT-54** (auto, Logic) [CT-21, TX-26, EC-CT-35] GIVEN `UI3_PLATE`, WHEN the Quit-without-saving hold completes, THEN `request_shutdown` is called exactly once, nothing is written, and input after the call is ignored.
- **AC-CT-55** (auto + auto-render, Logic + Visual) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* [CT-17, D.7, SL UI-2, C13; qa-lead R20] GIVEN `SaveInfo.playtime` of 9641, 0, 59 and 360000, THEN the **main-menu Continue line** reads `02:40:41`, `00:00:00`, `00:00:59` and `100:00:00`; it is read on every `MAIN_MENU` entry (re-read after a FAULTED return, a failed `new_game` or a Display & Comfort return, TX-34, TX-37; never refreshed while the menu stays open) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (WHEN the `SaveInfo` double's playtime changes while the menu is open, THEN the line does not change); **the pause panel shows no save fact**: its draw list (headless) and, in the render half (auto-render, masked to the panel plate), its pixels are identical across 0, 1 and 3 autosave periods and with the gate open or deferring (AC-SL-133); GIVEN the `SaveInfo` double is replaced and the layer returns to `MAIN_MENU` (a FAULTED return or a failed `new_game`), THEN the Continue line is re-read and shows the new playtime *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, and it never shows the playtime, `depth_label`, a "time since", a countdown, a gate state or a per-write mark *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; follows SL UI-2 as amended)*.
- **AC-CT-56** (auto + manual, UI, BLOCKING) [CT-23, SL UI-4; qa-lead R9] GIVEN the main menu, THEN the items are Continue, New campaign, Display & Comfort, Quit in that order *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; Display & Comfort is AC-CT-122)*; Continue is disabled (not hidden) when `campaign_info()` is empty, and default focus is Continue if enabled else New campaign; the Continue line contains exactly: playtime, crew count, kind word and `RING <n>`, and **never** the `RISKY` word whatever `SaveInfo` holds *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, (the newest record's ring, never a maximum; AC-CT-113); the screen has no thumbnail, no completion figure, no wall-clock "last played", no title art, no logo animation, no tagline, no seed field and no generation or chain chooser; the backdrop is the depth-ring plate and ring-1 filament sheet with zero node glyphs (fallback plain Ground). ### F. Negative requirements (one criterion per rule, each citing its source)

Criteria AC-CT-61 to AC-CT-64 and AC-CT-67 are checked in two parts: the **element whitelist** of AC-CT-107 proves the layer's scenes contain no member of the forbidden class (so a reviewer's silence is not the only evidence), and the **palette scan** of AC-CT-69 proves no Violet, Flesh or Alarm Red pixel; a reviewer then signs the retained captures.

- **AC-CT-61** (auto whitelist + review, UI, BLOCKING) [CT-31, AT:1373, AC-AT-61, AC-AT-66; qa-lead R7] GIVEN every screen of the layer at both sanity stages (in-session screens) and with every accessibility toggle, THEN no Attention indicator, meter, tier, hint or glyph appears; no legend, number, tier word, colour change or animation is attached to any ring and the tones are constant across Attention values 0 to max (AC-CT-41); there is no per-write autosave mark.
- **AC-CT-62** (auto whitelist + review, UI, BLOCKING) [CT-32, HE:377, HE:1358, AC-HE-119] GIVEN every screen under every accessibility setting, THEN no horror, threat bar, proximity indicator, direction, off-screen arrow, countdown, "last seen" pin or target ring is in the whitelist inventory or the captures, and this criterion is listed in the UX specs' own criteria.
- **AC-CT-63** (auto whitelist + review, UI, BLOCKING) [CT-33, BS:334, AC-BS-47c] GIVEN the chart with an UNSEEN, a SEEN and a BOARDED derelict, THEN no loot value and no remaining-salvage count appears anywhere on the layer.
- **AC-CT-64** (auto whitelist + review, UI, BLOCKING) [CT-34, SN:1350, SN:1357, AC-SN-83] GIVEN every in-session screen at stage 0 and forced stage 3, THEN no sanity meter, bar, ring, number, icon or colour is in the whitelist inventory or the captures, including under accessibility settings; the captures are retained.
- **AC-CT-65** (auto + review, UI, BLOCKING) [CT-35, CT-14, WM:1049, C1] GIVEN the string table and every captured chart state, THEN no fuel figure, travel time, ETA, danger rating, distance label, name beside a node or "recommended" marker exists; the only chart text is `IN TRANSIT`, control hints and (compact only) the CT-44 neutral ordinals; the chart is not changed by any fuel input (AC-CT-24).
- **AC-CT-66** (review + lint CI job to be built, Config) [CT-36, SL H5, SL:965, SL-24] GIVEN the layer, THEN no per-write autosave mark, thumbnail, spinner, percentage, "progress saved" toast, loading text, tip or death screen exists, and no asset by those names is in the layer's asset list.

- **AC-CT-114** (auto, Logic, BLOCKING) [CT-3 re-open governor, EC-CT-68, EC-CT-77 to 79, TX-13, TX-18, HR-13] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; new)* GIVEN a `ClockDouble`, a `KernelDouble` and `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS` 334: **114a** WHEN `chart_table` is pressed with no lower in the preceding 334 ms, THEN the panel and `request_pause` occur on that frame. **114b** WHEN a close occurs at `t` and `chart_table` is pressed at `t + 100 ms`, THEN the layer stays CLOSED and the kernel stays `RUNNING` until `t + 334 ms`, and on the first frame at or after it the plate appears and `request_pause` is called on the same frame (never the plate without the call, never the call without the plate); the key may be released earlier and the raise still applies. **114c** WHEN a second `chart_table` press arrives while a raise is queued, THEN the queue is unchanged, the raise still applies at the interval end and no extra call is made, and `UI_CHART_REOPEN_QUEUED` was requested once, at the first press, not at the second (AC-CT-138) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2/G6: CT-3 queued acknowledgement, ASK-CT-20)*; WHEN `cancel` arrives while a raise is queued, THEN the queue is cancelled, the layer stays at its level and no pause is requested *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **114d** WHEN `chart_table` is pressed ten times at 100 ms spacing starting from CLOSED, THEN every close is applied on its press frame and at most three raises occur in any rolling 1 s window, the kernel `PAUSED` state and the plate agreeing on every frame (the assertion is on the pair, per frame). **114e** WHEN Chart is accepted within 334 ms of a `cancel` from the chart, THEN the replacement is queued with the panel still showing; a restore clears the queue (TX-27); a console press with a valid pick drops it (EC-CT-78); the same governor admits TX-18 (a console-opened chart) and no raise is exempt.
- **AC-CT-115** (auto-render, Visual, BLOCKING) [CT-3, CT-VA-15, registry `flash_count` and `FLASH_MAX_PER_S`, Pipeline PR-13] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; new; the "no element faster than 3 Hz" claim made measurable)* GIVEN a per-frame linear-luminance log (`Y = 0.2126 R + 0.7152 G + 0.0722 B` of linear RGB, the Pipeline's definition) over the **layer-3 rectangle and the frame mean**, captured by the auto-render job at 60 fps for 60 s on two fixtures: **(F1) a bright lamp-lit world** (a retained still whose region under the layer-3 rectangle has a mean `Y` large enough that opening the Ground plate crosses the 0.10 `Y_scale` transition threshold) and **(F2) an active held-ON warning-mark fixture** (the world with a HUD warning mark active and its phase OFF at the moment of the first pause, so every pause cycle costs a mark leading edge) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: re-derived against HUD HS-23, the mark is held ON solid while paused and, at the first-launch default (reduce flashing ON), is held solid at all times, so F2 produces mark edges **only with reduce flashing off**; F2 is therefore run with reduce flashing off (where the pause adds at most one OFF-to-ON edge per pause and the resume bridge adds none) and, separately, with it on (where the mark adds zero edges and the capture must show the layer-3 rectangle alone at `F <= 2`), WHEN each fixture runs the scripted inputs {Esc mash at 100 ms spacing for 10 s, a Chart open/cancel mash for 10 s, a console-press chart open/cancel mash for 10 s, a held-direction run on the chart for 10 s, an idle 10 s}, once with reduce flashing off and once on, THEN registry `flash_count` (`F = ceil(n_transitions / 2)` over any sliding 1 s window) is **`F <= 3` for the layer-3 rectangle, for each 3 x 3 grid cell and for the frame mean, with the HUD mark's edges counted in the same capture**, and `F <= 2` with reduce flashing on; the captures and the per-frame logs are retained. If F2 fails at 334 ms, `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS` is raised within 334-1000 and the criterion re-run before the story closes.

Variants: close at 0, press at 100, `cancel` at 150 THEN 1 cue, no pause, the layer stays closed; a press with no lower in the preceding interval THEN 0 cues; press at 100 then a console press with no valid pick (a different desired raise, TX-18) at 200 THEN 2 cues; queue then `request_restore` THEN the queue is cleared and no further cue.
- **AC-CT-139** (auto, Logic, BLOCKING) [CT-21, TX-23, TX-38, TX-39, EC-CT-38, OQ-CT-37] *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6)* GIVEN `quit_write_active` goes false to true in each of `CLOSED`, `PANEL`, `CHART` and `DISPLAY_COMFORT`, THEN the layer enters `SAVE_QUIT_WAIT` (TX-39) with every item disabled, nothing focusable, `chart_table` and `cancel` ignored, nothing added to the screen, and from `CLOSED` nothing drawn and gameplay input not intercepted; GIVEN `save_status` `WRITING` with `quit_write_active` false (an autosave), THEN the draw list and state are unchanged; GIVEN `quit_write_active` ends with `WRITTEN`, THEN the layer calls nothing (TX-22); GIVEN `FAILED` + `quit_held`, THEN TX-23 (from `CLOSED` in SOLO the panel is raised and `request_pause` called once); GIVEN `SL_QUIT_NO_WRITE_DOWNED` from a window close, THEN `UI3_PLATE` (TX-38) and Back / `cancel` returns to `CLOSED` with one `request_resume` and exactly one `cancel_held_close()` call; GIVEN Resume or `chart_table` from `SAVE_FAILED` with `quit_held` true, THEN one `cancel_held_close()`; GIVEN the exit ends in the Quit hold, THEN none.

- **AC-CT-133** (lint CI job to be built, Config, BLOCKING) [Coverage matrix] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review; new)* GIVEN this document, WHEN a generated check extracts every `AC-CT-n` (with its letter suffix) defined in the Acceptance Criteria section and every id (including expanded "a to b" ranges) in the coverage matrix, THEN no defined id is missing from the matrix; the check runs on every change to this file and its first run is the one that found the rows added by the re-review.
- **AC-CT-134** (lint CI job to be built, Config, BLOCKING) [D.1d, CT-20, CT-28] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review; new)* GIVEN the notice paragraph and the boot-flow string set, WHEN the fit lint of D.1d runs at the smallest reachable boot canvas (row 5, `H = 3`) and at the forced-`H` row 6, THEN the greedy-wrapped paragraph is within the line limit of the row (16 lines at row 5, 9 at row 6 at the estimated advance, re-run on the real face), the three notice controls and the settings-line slot fit `V_boot`, and the main-menu rail fits (73 <= 216 at row 5).

## Open Questions

*Owner is `user` (a product decision nobody else may take), `agent` (a specialist, neighbour or lead can answer) or `GDD` (this document answers it in a later revision). Target is `playtest`, `next GDD` (a named neighbour) or `ADR`. Each row states the **default this GDD already assumes**, so nothing here blocks implementation. User-decision rows come first. The ASK-CT rows (provisional seams to neighbours) live in Cross-References and are not repeated; rows closed by a ruling are listed last so their ids are not reused. Rows resolved or defaulted by the 2026-10-27 user rulings (OQ-CT-1, 7, 8, 28, 30, 31, 32, 33) keep their place and are marked in their text; on 2026-10-28 the pending-confirmation defaults (OQ-CT-1, 8, 30, 32) are confirmed by delegated ruling R20 and the remaining user-owned rows (OQ-CT-4, 6) are decided by delegated ruling R21, so no user-decision row is open *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated rulings R20, R21)*.*

| # | Question (default assumed) | Owner | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ-CT-1 | Default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20): the Display & Comfort page carries a **master volume** at MVP (D85): a fourth control, a stepped row of `VOLUME_STEPS` notches published through the `SettingsStore`; Adaptive Audio owns the bus, the step-to-gain law and the default (CT-18, CT-29, D.1c). Original question: should the page carry a master volume at MVP? The earlier default was none, reconsidered in the design review because Display & Comfort is now on the main menu (CT-23) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | user (confirmed, R20) | closed |
| OQ-CT-4 | Is a Records page (a list of campaigns) and an un-retire screen wanted before Alpha? *Default: no.* At MVP a retired file stays on disk and is recoverable by file only (CT-24); Retire is now held at `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` so the act that the player experiences as irreversible costs a hold *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (OQ-CT-39). Resolved 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21 (user delegated all remaining calls): the default stands, no Records page or un-retire screen before Alpha, because it preserves every prior ruling with no new content *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21)* | user (decided, R21) | closed |
